Stash course project metadata contains `semio.preflight` with Debian 12/x64 Linux requirements, minimumAvailableBytes 77309411328, minimumFreeLoops 7, tools fallocate/mkfs.ext4/mount/umount/losetup/cc/pkg-config/docker.
